# Current State

## Latest Continuation — #336 Live-reliability fix 1/3: assessment_planner respects autonomous_intrusive_allowed (2026-05-19)

First of the 3 live-reliability root-cause fixes from the 2026-05-18 Gruyere diagnosis (run `9f951a0f` produced 0/17 known Gruyere classes despite the operator opting into intrusive). Root cause traced to `_score_capability` lines 277-279: `if risk_tier in INTRUSIVE_TIERS: decision = "NEEDS_APPROVAL"` — the function never consulted `run.autonomous_intrusive_allowed`. The picker (Claude Opus) read the correct "INTRUSIVE TIER AUTHORIZED" block in `_AI_PICKER_SYSTEM_PROMPT` and tried to plan exec_plan / impacket / metasploit chains, but the statically-scored planner overruled it. Result: 0 exec_plan picks in 24 reasonings, picker fell back to random residual tools (ai-shell / c2-ingest / graphql / iot-default-creds), run died on `max_iterations_reached`.

Fix: added `intrusive_allowed: bool = False` kwarg to `_score_capability`; threaded `run.autonomous_intrusive_allowed` through `build_assessment_plan_for_run` to every scoring call. When intrusive-tier AND allowed, the `if risk_tier in INTRUSIVE_TIERS:` gate is skipped and the decision falls through to the SAME evidence-driven branches (sqlmap / jwt / deser / matched_blockers / matched_triggers / weak_triggers / WEB_SIGNALS / default) that active/passive runners use. Audit-trail: new `auto_allowed_intrusive: bool` field on each plan-item dict so operators see which decisions used the bypass. `_decision_sentence` deliberately untouched — once `decision` is RUN/WAIT/SKIP the existing templates fire correctly.

8 new tests in `test_assessment_planner.py` under "#336 / backlog-398" section: regression guard (not-allowed → NEEDS_APPROVAL unchanged), acceptance (allowed+triggered → RUN), blocker-wins (allowed+blocker → SKIP), weak-triggers (allowed+weak → WAIT), no-evidence (allowed+empty → SKIP), non-intrusive-no-op (active/passive kwarg ignored), backward-compat (default kwarg preserves NEEDS_APPROVAL), source-grep sentinel (`build_assessment_plan_for_run` MUST thread the flag).

11/13 pass in test_assessment_planner.py. The 2 pre-existing `test_adaptive_wave_completion_*` failures verified independent of #336 via `git stash` baseline (tracked under backlog-343 broader codebase hygiene). The 5 `test_assessment_loop.py` `running` vs `waiting_for_execution` status-drift failures are also pre-existing (same pattern documented under #330/#331/#335/#344). ruff clean; compileall clean; git diff --check clean. Constraints honored: `INTRUSIVE_TIERS` unchanged, `_AI_PICKER_SYSTEM_PROMPT` untouched, approval workflows preserved for non-allowed runs.

**Live test deferred to backlog-399/400 follow-up**: this code change alone needs to combine with backlog-399 (auth_discoverer expansion) + backlog-400 (probe path-augmentation) before a fair live Gruyere re-test is possible. The picker's exec_plan choice depends on evidence that the other two bugs currently block from surfacing.

**NEXT_TASK promoted to backlog-399 (#337 — auth_discoverer: discover register/login from target_intel hints + index link-scan)** — second of the 3 live-reliability fixes.

## Earlier — #346 Backfill: Auth-recipe cache eviction + schema migration (2026-05-18)

Fifth of the 6 deferred-capability follow-ups (#342-#347). #327 declared "store rejects when dir cap engages" as a success criterion — in production that means once the 50 KB cache fills up, NEW fingerprints get an absent verdict forever and the pipeline pays the Claude Haiku cost on every run. Same dead-weight problem on `schema_version` mismatch: `_load_cache_entry` returned None (absent) but no migration path existed, so a schema bump would orphan every prior entry. This commit adds: (a) `_evict_for_space(needed_bytes, *, now, keep_path=None)` — bounded LRU helper, sorts candidates by (anchor_timestamp_asc, confidence_asc), caps eviction at `int(0.25 * len(candidates))` per call, aborts at pure ties so no arbitrary choices; (b) integration in `store_recipe`'s cap-breach branch: instead of returning False, request `needed = projected - cap` bytes of eviction, only fail if `freed < needed`; (c) `_migrate_entry(entry, *, target_schema_version)` + `_RECIPE_MIGRATIONS` registry — idempotent on already-current entries (identity-preserved), chain-walker for forward versions (registry empty today by design, ready when v2 ships), unknown versions return None + log a single WARNING per (fingerprint_hash, process) via `_MIGRATION_WARNED_FINGERPRINTS` set with a test seam `_reset_migration_warning_cache()`; (d) `_load_cache_entry` swap: schema-version check delegated to `_migrate_entry` so every read site benefits (lookup_recipe, store_recipe's pre-overwrite read, mark_recipe_stale). 9 new tests in `test_auth_discoverer_cache.py`: eviction order (oldest first), 25% bound, lower-confidence tie-break, pure-tie abort, end-to-end full-cache pure-tie store returns False, migration idempotent, unknown-version single-warning-per-fingerprint, cross-fingerprint warning independence, lookup_recipe end-to-end on a v9999 entry. 1 existing test rewritten (`test_store_rejects_when_dir_cap_breached` → `test_store_evicts_when_dir_cap_breached`) because its assertion captured the #327 contract that #346 deliberately replaces. 91/91 pass across all 4 auth_discoverer test files. ruff clean; compileall clean; git diff --check clean. Constraints honored: cap constants / verdict vocabulary / build_recipe / 5 probes / memory_backend public API all untouched. **NEXT_TASK promoted to backlog-397 (#347 — Empirical Phase-3 live validation)** — sixth and FINAL of the 6 backfills, #330/#332/#333 follow-up. After #347 the queue returns to backlog-386 (#336 Non-HTTP Primitives SMB+LDAP) for Phase 4 day 3.

## Earlier — #345 Backfill: Operator-credentialed auth path for login-only targets (2026-05-18)

Fourth of the 6 deferred-capability follow-ups (#342-#347). #329 post-merge addendum explicitly flagged login-only targets as a Phase 2.5/4 enhancement worth filing but never did. acquire_session did emergent registration only — real-world targets (DVWA, customer staging, internal corporate apps) without a register flow silently no-op'd. This commit adds: (a) `_lookup_operator_credentials(session, *, run, base_url)` that queries `run_credential_vault` for a `password`-kind secret whose `metadata['target']` host matches base_url, decrypting via the existing `secrets.decrypt_secret_value` API; (b) `_login_only_fingerprint(fp)` that strips register_form so `build_recipe` produces a login-only recipe via its existing prompt (no build_recipe changes per #326 contract); (c) `acquire_session_with_credentials(base_url, *, username, password, ...)` that runs the same fetch+fingerprint+execute pipeline but with operator credentials and login-only fingerprint (cache skipped, namespacing is backlog-396); (d) two new optional kwargs `session=None, run=None` on `acquire_session()` that dispatch to the credentialed variant on vault hit and fall through to emergent otherwise. The 5 #328 probes that call `acquire_session(base, scope_allowlist=...)` see no behavior change — defaults preserve the existing path. Returned session dict omits `password` key per credential_handling hygiene; greppable test enforces no leakage. 17 new tests in `test_auth_discoverer_credentialed.py` (host extraction / fingerprint stripping / happy path / login-fails / no-login-form / empty-input / 6 vault matching scenarios / 4 dispatch scenarios incl. probe-contract sentinel + leakage greppable). 99/99 pass across all 5 auth_discoverer test files. ruff clean; compileall clean; git diff --check clean. AGENT_SYSTEM_MAP §3 updated with the "Operator-credentialed strategy" paragraph + last_verified 2026-05-18. **NEXT_TASK promoted to backlog-396 (#346 — Auth-recipe cache eviction + schema migration)** — fifth backfill, #327 follow-up. Closes the silent-rejection-when-cache-full gap.

## Earlier — #344 Backfill: Verifier hook moved to pre-persistence (2026-05-18)

Third of the 6 deferred-capability follow-ups (#342-#347). #335 wired the verifier as a post-advance hook in `assessment_loop.py` — findings were persisted with original severity, then downgraded on the next advance. Brief visibility window where consumers (UI Mission Control, exporters, webhooks) could see un-verified critical severity. This commit moves the call into `workers/orchestration/findings.store_findings`: new `_verify_pre_persistence(*, finding, run)` helper runs the verifier BEFORE `session.add` + `session.flush`, mutates `created_finding.severity` IN PLACE if the verdict says failed (so the row hits the DB with the already-downgraded severity AND the audit event picks it up too), then `apply_verifier_verdict` attaches the verdict dict to `FindingAiEnrichment.metadata_json['verifier']` after flush. The `assessment_loop.py` post-advance hook is left in place as a self-no-opping defensive backstop (`verify_pending_critical_findings` already skips findings with `metadata_json['verifier']` set). 8 new tests in `test_finding_verifier.py`: below-threshold skip, missing-run defensive guard, severity mutation on failed verdict, double-downgrade guard (mid-merge state), confirmed-no-change, exception swallow (graceful_degradation), source-grep sentinel on `_verify_pre_persistence(` + `apply_verifier_verdict(` literals in `store_findings`, source-grep sentinel keeping the assessment_loop backstop alive. 42/42 pass (28 existing #335 + 6 chain smoke + 8 new). ruff clean (auto-fixed pre-existing I001 import sort in findings.py since I added `import logging`). Constraints honored: verifier strategies/dataclass/constants/metadata layout/finding-schema all untouched per #335 contract carried forward. Pre-existing `test_worker_ai_loop_advances_*` failures verified unrelated via `git stash` baseline. **NEXT_TASK promoted to backlog-395 (#345 — Operator-credentialed auth path for login-only targets)** — fourth backfill, #328/#329 follow-up. Closes the emergent-registration-only gap so the auth_discoverer pipeline works on DVWA-style targets where login is the only available flow.

## Earlier — Mythos Phase 4 Day 2: Cross-Runner Verifier (2026-05-17)

Mythos task #335 (backlog-385 — Cross-Runner Verifier: Independent Confirmation for Critical Findings) completed. New `api/app/services/finding_verifier.py` (~430 lines) introduces a service that, for each newly-arrived critical/high finding, runs a SINGLE bounded HTTP check via a DIFFERENT code path than the originating runner (zero runner imports — structural independence) and either confirms the impact, downgrades the severity (one notch per ladder `critical→high, high→medium`) with a verifier_note, or marks the verdict inconclusive. Three strategies: `admin_access` (anonymous GET checks for generic admin keywords like "manage this server" / "administrator panel" / "all users"), `stored_xss` (extracts AEGISXSS marker from summary, asserts reflection on re-fetch), `info_disclosure` (re-fetches leaky URL, checks generic leak indicators — quoted-key password, private-key block, /etc/passwd format, phpinfo marker). `VerifierVerdict` dataclass + `apply_verifier_verdict()` writes the verdict to `FindingAiEnrichment.metadata_json["verifier"]` (no finding-schema change; uses existing extensible mechanism). Idempotent — re-applying a failed verdict does NOT double-downgrade (current severity must equal verdict.original_severity). Integration hook in `assessment_loop.py:advance_assessment_loop` before the narrator block calls `verify_pending_critical_findings(session, run=run, scope=scope, limit=10)` wrapped in try/except (enrichment, not gating). 34 unit tests pass (28 verifier + 6 new finding_chains baseline smoke tests). ruff clean. Constraints: workers/runners/ + workers/orchestration/ + finding schema + system prompt all untouched. Per `feedback_no_prescriptive_recipes`: admin keyword catalog is generic, no Gruyere seeds. **NEXT_TASK promoted to backlog-386 (#336 Non-HTTP Primitives: SMB and LDAP as exec_plan Steps)** — Phase 4 day 3: extends exec_plan grammar with `smb_request` and `ldap_request` step kinds for cross-protocol attack composition (web → SMB / web → LDAP chains in one continuous narrative).

## Earlier — Mythos Phase 3 Day 2: AI Loop Soft Retry (2026-05-16)

Mythos task #331 (backlog-381 — AI Loop Soft Retry on Parse / Decision Failure Instead of Hard Stop) completed. New `_call_picker_with_retries` wrapper in `api/app/services/assessment_loop.py` (right before `_claude_select_next_action`) iterates `range(PICKER_MAX_RETRIES + 1)` = 3 total attempts. On entry: resets `loop_state["retries_this_advance"] = 0` and `loop_state["picker_retry_exhausted"] = False`. On a None from the picker: increments counter, logs INFO, continues. On a non-None: returns the decision (and logs "picker recovered after N retry(ies)" if N > 0). On full exhaustion: logs WARNING, sets `picker_retry_exhausted = True`, returns None. `PICKER_MAX_RETRIES = 2` module constant. `advance_assessment_loop` wired through the wrapper; the existing `if candidate is None` branch now branches the stop_reason: `picker_retry_exhausted` (operator can see Claude died on every retry) vs `no_registered_runner_candidate` (no fallback heuristic applicable). Audit event metadata includes `retries_this_advance` int for telemetry. 9 new unit tests in `test_assessment_loop_soft_retry.py` (happy path + 1-fail recovery + 2-fail recovery edge + 3-fail exhaustion + stale-state reset + log assertions + budget-constant sentinel + empty-dict-is-still-decision). 17/17 structured-output tests from #330 still pass; 5 pre-existing test_assessment_loop.py failures unchanged from baseline. ruff clean; compileall clean. **NEXT_TASK promoted to backlog-382 (#332 Memory Tool Result Truncation and Bloat Containment)** — Phase 3 day 3, closes the unbounded `/memories/run-progress.md` issue and unlocks ~$30/day projected saving from capping 11 KB-avg memory tool reads at 2 KB.

Four new public functions on `api/app/services/auth_discoverer.py`: `lookup_recipe(fp) -> (recipe|None, verdict)` returning fresh/stale/absent verdicts, `store_recipe(fp, recipe, confidence)` enforcing 2 KB per-file and 50 KB total dir caps, `mark_recipe_stale(fp)` for replay-failure flagging, and `verify_recipe(recipe, http_client)` that replays only the verification step before trusting a cached recipe. ## Architecture

AegisPT currently consists of:

- a FastAPI control plane
- SQLAlchemy models with Alembic migrations
- evidence-first runs, tasks, artifacts, findings, and report generation
- Prompt 1 methodology alignment across PTES, OWASP WSTG, NIST SP 800-115, and optional ASVS references
- Prompt 2 adaptive testing support through inventory, threat intelligence, relevance analysis, capability packs, validation, approval, deployment, and runtime pack binding
- a Prompt 3 Codex build lane that adds supervised pack refinement context, repository governance, and repo-backed Codex task management

AegisPT now computes a deterministic continuous exposure drift report when run drift is computed, persists it under `Run.configuration_json.continuous_exposure_drift`, and exposes it through `GET /api/v1/runs/{run_id}/continuous-exposure-drift`. The report compares the current run with the previous completed/completed_with_errors run for the same engagement/profile using existing run data only: open finding fingerprints, `InventorySnapshot.normalized_inventory_json`, latest `TargetContextDocument`, finding IDs/evidence refs, and existing exposure scoring. It detects new/removed services and ports, new/removed endpoints, technology additions/removals/version changes including downgrade direction, target-context drift, new/resolved finding drift, and emits SOC/operator alerts for potential shadow IT, unauthorized exposure drift, patch regression, new findings, and resolved findings. - backlog-298 `One-Click Proof of Resolution Micro-Scanner` is complete. AegisPT now exposes `POST /api/v1/findings/{finding_id}/proof-of-resolution/micro-scan`, which requires an existing `Finding.replay_recipe_json` before it will queue proof work. The micro-scan service checks the current approved scope, selects an existing RunProfile for the registered tool, creates a proof-of-resolution run, links a `FindingValidationRecord`, and keeps active profiles behind the existing run approval flow. `queue_run_tasks` now applies the exact stored replay task params for proof-of-resolution runs, including approval-triggered task queueing after a pending active run is approved. When a proof micro-scan finalizes with `validated_not_reproduced`, the validator treats that as successful controlled proof: it marks the finding `resolved`, remediation `verified`, clears `sla_breached`, and writes audit evidence. If the evidence pattern still reproduces, the finding remains open and the failed proof is audited. No new runner was added, no UI was touched, and no runner registry, approval, scope, ROE, credential, provenance, runtime-binding, report, or kill-switch gate was bypassed. `http://127.0.0.1:8000` browser validation was not required for this backend/API/runtime queue slice; live API smoke ran on temporary port `http://127.0.0.1:8028` to avoid disturbing any shared local server. - backlog-260 `AI Continuous Assessment Loop` is complete as a backend/API-only controller. AegisPT now exposes `GET /api/v1/runs/{run_id}/assessment-loop` and `POST /api/v1/runs/{run_id}/assessment-loop/iterate` for bounded `ai_loop` runs. The loop builds AssessmentState from approved targets, inventory/SignalProfile, findings, tested paths, open proposals, active loop tasks, and target type; stores state under `run.configuration_json.ai_assessment_loop`; and creates one NextAction per iteration with exact tool, target, params, tier, reasoning, confidence, follow-up hints, expected evidence, and approval metadata. URL scope seeds `httpx`, CIDR/IP scope seeds `nmap`, and domain scope can seed `easm-discover` without creating a fixed-phase assessment chain. Passive/low-impact zero-approval actions create queued registered-runner `Task` records with `queued_not_dispatched` provenance only; active and intrusive actions create pending review-only `AgentProposal` records, so existing approval/scope/ROE/credential/runtime binding/provenance/report/kill-switch controls remain authoritative. The loop refuses to advance while selected-run approvals are pending or loop tasks are queued/running, stops after the configured no-new-signal threshold, and enforces the default 20-iteration cap. Fixed-phase/manual chains remain available; the loop records `manual_chain_alternative_preserved=true` and `fixed_phase_chains_replaced_globally=false`. `http://127.0.0.1:8000` was not relevant because no UI or served runtime asset changed. The original package included old UI acceptance wording, but the user explicitly directed that old UI work should be removed because the UI will be rebuilt; backlog-264 and backlog-265 were archived under `codex/tasks/archived-old-ui/`, and `NEXT_TASK.md` now promotes backlog-268 `React v2 foundation`. A later GitHub-only failure in run `25218967770` showed Playwright Chromium was installed outside the path used by the fast UI test step; CI now sets `PLAYWRIGHT_BROWSERS_PATH` at workflow job scope so installation and tests use the same browser location. GitHub run `25219458445` then exposed a synthetic AI Signal smoke-test race where real browser SSE could overwrite injected stream text; that test now disables real `EventSource` only inside the synthetic state injection. Evidence is recorded in `codex/results/github-ci-pr-gate-correction-20260501.md`. Local validation passed for the new backend CI lane, fast UI lane, backlog validator, `git diff --check`, and targeted AI/Nuclei/Semgrep regressions. - 2026-05-01 enterprise-readiness audit/backlog-curation update: the operator requested a careful user-perspective system analysis for enterprise readiness. The current active task remains backlog-157 `Fix Jira 404 console error fired on every findings drawer open when Jira not configured`; no implementation task was promoted or bypassed. Required repo-truth files were reread, the active task package was checked, and `python3 scripts/validate_backlog_tasks.py` confirmed exactly one active task before audit work. Live localhost was relevant. The first audit attempt exposed a real reliability issue: the inline host could remain bound to `127.0.0.1:8000` while uvicorn was in `Shutting down` / `Waiting for connections to close`, causing `/health/live` and Playwright navigation timeouts. After a controlled restart with `AEGISPT_LOCAL_ENSURE_ZAP=0 bash scripts/restart_local_inline_host.sh`, `/health/live` returned healthy JSON and `scripts/run_full_system_acceptance_audit.py` completed, visiting `13/14` screens and recording `42` defects in `codex/results/full-system-enterprise-readiness-audit-20260501.{json,md}`. The defects were mostly already mapped to existing backlog-151/152/153 work: unreachable Dashboard navigation, wizard/internal copy, Settings/Custom CVE/Pack Forge/History field guidance gaps, Pack Forge disabled actions without unlock reasons, and `nan`/placeholder copy. `scripts/run_enterprise_ia_audit.py` still failed with Playwright `networkidle` timeout, likely because app polling prevents a stable network-idle state; this is now captured by the new runtime/audit resilience task. Seven non-duplicate enterprise-readiness gaps were added as queued tasks backlog-181 through backlog-187: local runtime liveness/audit resilience, enterprise SSO/session/identity administration, backup/restore/upgrade rollback drill, observability/support diagnostics bundle, accessibility/keyboard enterprise UX gate, enterprise deployment hardening, and enterprise release-readiness/support-claim ledger. `python3 scripts/validate_backlog_tasks.py` now passes with `187` queued task packages mapped one-to-one and exactly one active task; `git diff --check` passes. A post-audit `/health/live` check timed out again, confirming the liveness issue remains real; the host was restarted once more and ended healthy for operator use. Remaining risk: this was an audit/backlog-curation pass, not product repair; the full-system audit is heuristic, IA audit remains blocked by `networkidle`, and the 42 recorded defects still require the existing/promoted future tasks to be implemented.

- Corrective assessment-chain recoverable failure auto-triage hotfix on 2026-04-28: assessment chains now distinguish critical baseline failure from recoverable downstream runner failure. `passive-network-discovery` failures still hard-block, but later runner failures such as web baseline or credential-capture errors are recorded as skipped chain gaps with `system:auto-triage`, failure metadata, and a deterministic `chain_runtime_failure_triage` AI Signal record before the chain advances to the remaining planned checks. Evidence is recorded in `codex/results/assessment-chain-recoverable-failure-auto-triage-hotfix-20260428.md`.
- Corrective wizard prerequisite / queued-start hotfix on 2026-04-28: the wizard preview now asks which access prerequisites are available before launch, covering the unauthenticated lane, AD/domain context, credential refs, hash/password material, intrusive LAN authorization, and exploit confirmation authorization. Those choices are stored in `configuration_json.access.prerequisites` and included in the mission confirmation summary. Mission Control no longer shows the confusing `Start Validation` action during already-running runs; the action is now labelled `Start Queued Checks` and appears only when the selected run itself is still `queued` or `requested`. UI assets are cache-busted with `20260428-wizard-prereq-queued-1`. Evidence is recorded in `codex/results/wizard-prerequisite-start-queued-hotfix-20260428.md`.
- Corrective Deep LAN unauthenticated enrichment hotfix on 2026-04-28: Full Internal LAN now does more useful work without usernames/passwords. Phase 1 is focused on discovery only, while Phase 2 now chains deeper unauthenticated enrichment after inventory exists: bounded nmap service enumeration with Deep LAN overrides, internal infrastructure audit, passive SMB/null-session review, LDAP enum against discovered LDAP services or configured domains, web baseline, nuclei audit, and AD/BloodHound only when prerequisites exist. The new `passive-lan-infrastructure-audit` profile reuses the existing infrastructure runner for full-port service detection, HTTP follow-up, SSH audit, passive SMB review, and infrastructure-focused nuclei validation against discovered internal hosts. LDAP enum can now discover RootDSE `defaultNamingContext` for IP targets when no `base_dn` was supplied. Evidence is recorded in `codex/results/deep-lan-unauthenticated-enrichment-hotfix-20260428.md`.
- Corrective ChatGPT OAuth session reuse hotfix on 2026-04-28: ChatGPT OAuth no longer requires a fresh OpenAI login for every engagement when an encrypted reusable session already exists. Status reports reusable sessions, authorize reuses the encrypted token set into the selected engagement as a separate encrypted `SecretRef`, Settings and wizard show `Use Existing ChatGPT Session`, and the real login popup is opened synchronously before async authorization to avoid browser popup blocking. Evidence is recorded in `codex/results/chatgpt-oauth-session-reuse-hotfix-20260428.md`.

## Implemented

- backlog-99 `Known-Vulnerability Validation Matrix - Does AegisPT Actually Find What It Claims To Find?` is complete: `scripts/validation/expected_findings.json` defines expected and forbidden finding patterns for WebGoat, DVWA, clean nginx, plus a disabled Metasploitable3 placeholder; `scripts/run_validation_matrix.py` loads that catalog, compares actual finding payloads in fast offline-fixture mode, optionally launches Docker targets and drives AegisPT via the existing acceptance/API flow, computes detection rate and false-positive rate, fails scenarios for missing expected findings or excess false positives, and writes `codex/results/validation-matrix-latest.json`; `.github/workflows/validation-matrix.yml` runs the validation tooling on schedule/dispatch/relevant PRs and uploads the result artifact with safe Slack notification on failure; `scripts/tests/test_validation_runner.py` covers matching, missing expected findings, false positives, forbidden findings, CLI output, and workflow YAML validity. Product code, pack registry, approval flow, deployment, and runtime binding were not changed. Validation passed with `TMPDIR=/tmp python3 -m pytest scripts/tests/test_validation_runner.py`, `python3 scripts/run_validation_matrix.py --catalog scripts/validation/expected_findings.json --results-file codex/results/validation-matrix-latest.json`, `python3 -m compileall scripts/run_validation_matrix.py scripts/tests/test_validation_runner.py`, `python3 scripts/validate_backlog_tasks.py`, and `git diff --check`.   - typed `Deep Assessment` and `Adaptive Customer-Specific Testing` answers on an explicit WAN IP/CIDR mission now stage the first run as `External Surface Only` / `passive-network-discovery`, skip the premature access question after scope, and persist `active_checks = passive-only`
  - targeted browser regression, static/API support checks, the broader UI matrix, and a fresh live-host Playwright proof now cover this path at `codex/results/live-host-wizard-external-wan-discovery-first-20260419.json`

## Adaptive testing status

- inventory normalization: implemented
- threat intelligence ingestion: implemented
- relevance analysis: implemented
- capability factory: implemented
- pack validation / approval / deployment / rollback: implemented
- runtime binding: implemented
- adaptive provenance in findings and reports: implemented

## Capability factory status

The capability factory is operational and should remain authoritative for pack generation. Codex-driven work must layer on top of existing generated packs, not replace the capability factory or its metadata.

## Codex build lane status

- Codex build lane context endpoints: implemented
- repository governance files: implemented
- repo-backed Codex task interface: implemented
- legacy repo-backed Codex task files are normalized for compatibility instead of breaking the task list endpoint
- safe export/validation wrappers: implemented, host-reproducible, and no longer blocked on `jq`
- sandbox: documented
- UI support requirements: documented and integrated into the primary operator console
- live runtime mutation from Codex: explicitly prohibited

## Known risks

- the repo has strong supported-path and matrix coverage, but it is not yet honest to describe the product as exhaustively end-to-end validated across every supported option and supported combination
- the current local baseline is stronger than before because the live host now proves HTML and PDF export again after restart, but broader supported-combination and broader lab breadth still remain open signoff work
- the market leaders are increasingly bundling remediation closure, attack-path prioritization, workflow integrations, and compliance-ready proof into one loop; if AegisPT does not operationalize those layers, it risks falling behind despite strong architecture
- reviewed workspace artifacts still need a controlled import path back into the pack artifact store
- local secret handling is intentionally baseline-grade and should be backed by an explicit deployment key or future vault integration in production
- browser validation currently targets Chromium only
- browser validation is now deterministic per test, but it is slower because each UI test gets an isolated temporary backend host
- wizard/browser coverage now includes conversational completion across every supported assessment type plus cross-surface adaptive-pack visibility, but it still focuses on Chromium and the repo-owned supported-path contract rather than every conceivable permutation
- Mission Control now exposes selected task focus without forcing Explorer first, but richer streaming telemetry is still future work beyond the current request/response run model
- the new product matrix covers the major feature/options surface, but it still does not exhaustively execute every third-party tool binary combination or every possible long-running lab topology
- deep internal-LAN missions now use a staged discovery-first operator path, but the later controlled internal-validation follow-up is still expressed through follow-up runs rather than a richer dedicated step-by-step scenario UI
- safe real-run acceptance is now controlled, but external lab availability still depends on the actual lab stack being present where the harness is executed
- WebGoat/crAPI are now proven on the current host, but any future active-safe lab still remains environment-dependent until its target stack and supporting services are live together where the harness runs
- execution detail is now easier to find in Mission Control and Explorer, but richer live telemetry and per-task streaming are still future enhancements
- inline dispatch is now much clearer for operators, but it is still a request/response completion model rather than true streaming runner telemetry
- the operator experience is materially stronger and now has an explicit supported-path signoff through Phase 7, but wider human-in-the-loop usability review on real environments is still valuable beyond automated coverage
- execution truth and LAN/internal follow-through are now materially stronger because discovery-derived follow-up runs keep visible lineage and next-step context across the main operator surfaces
- queued task dispatch remains serial in the current runtime path: `dispatch_task_batch()` iterates task ids one at a time, and inline mode blocks until each task finishes; true parallel execution still needs an explicit future dispatch/worker-capacity foundation that preserves approvals, kill switch behavior, target scope, rate/concurrency limits, audit, and runtime binding guarantees
- enterprise LAN coverage is not complete: the current supported LAN path is staged discovery/service enumeration via `nmap`, with a separate read-only `passive-identity-ldap-enum` profile available for LDAP/AD inventory, but Metasploit, Responder, BloodHound/SharpHound-style graph collection, CrackMapExec/NetExec-style SMB/Kerberos coverage, poisoning, credential attacks, and exploitation are not active product capabilities and require explicit approval-gated future task packages
- broader auth-family permutations beyond the currently proven credentialed-secret, manual-auth / SSO, API-token/bearer, and session/cookie routes, plus blocked/failed-family permutations and deeper adaptive-pack permutations, are now non-blocking future expansions rather than hidden signoff gaps
- the narrow/mobile wizard path is now materially stronger on the supported host because the confirmation handoff no longer competes with a squeezed two-column intake shell, but broader human calmness review across longer day-to-day mobile and tablet use is still open
- quick-reply trust is now materially stronger because representative suggested-answer flows are no longer only implied by the UI, but broader natural-language phrasing breadth beyond the current representative chip and typed paths still remains future signoff work
- summary calmness is now materially stronger because the still-needed card and the inline progress note no longer repeat the same topic through competing action chips, linked-topic badges, and duplicate progress labels, but broader human calmness review across the full wizard still remains open
- opening-intake calmness is now materially stronger because the very first wizard screen no longer claims downstream `Access` / `Output` obligations before they are actually relevant, but broader human calmness review across the full wizard still remains open
- portal-intent trust is now materially stronger because a common operator phrase like `Our customer portal` no longer routes into the wrong generic mission type, but broader human calmness review across the full wizard still remains open
- TLS-only goal trust is now materially stronger because pure certificate/protocol missions no longer invite impossible deep/adaptive promises, but broader human calmness review across the full wizard still remains open
- advanced access handoff is now materially stronger because the wizard exposes a real next action instead of a quiet conversational loop, but broader human signoff on the overall authenticated-setup calmness is still open
- Phase 1 wizard trust now has a supported-path signoff packet across API, browser, live-host, and manual operator review for the current assessment-type matrix; broader day-to-day calmness review is still worth continuing, but it no longer blocks the broader optional backlog that follows the completed operator-console stabilization program
- advanced override synchronization is now stronger because the visible question focus follows the real deterministic run model after drawer changes and invalid advanced input no longer produces a fake confirm-ready dead end, but broader human operator calmness/signoff is still open
- fresh live-host proof now also pins the current representative advanced-override truth:
  - the web route that starts on `Standard assessment` and is then pushed to `Deep` plus active/deep safety in the drawer now realigns from `Safety` to `Boundaries`, not to an auth prompt
  - on the supported host that route persists `active-web-zap-active`, while the separate internal-validation-to-web scope-revision route still re-prompts `Boundaries` and lands on `active-web-safe`
- dossier alignment is now stronger because the planned-mission summary is no longer transient, but broader Phase 1 human review on clarity/calmness and day-to-day trust is still open
- dossier-route truth is now stronger because existing-engagement reuse remains visible after mission creation instead of becoming implicit, but broader Phase 1 human review on clarity/calmness and day-to-day trust is still open
- new-dossier route truth is now stronger because creation intent and the chosen dossier name stay visible and stable through mission creation, but broader Phase 1 human review on clarity/calmness and day-to-day trust is still open
- authorization-context truth is now stronger because dossier `Customer` / `ROE Reference` metadata is aligned centrally in the API instead of being truthful only when the browser wizard happened to populate it, but broader Phase 1 human review on clarity/calmness and day-to-day trust is still open
- scope-guardrail truth is now stronger because advanced scope exclusions no longer leave a stale `boundary_handling` default in the stored run dossier, but broader Phase 1 human review on clarity/calmness and day-to-day trust is still open

- rich-route boundary truth is now stronger because mixed/adaptive and access-aware routes no longer infer `Standard Scope Only` silently after `Safety`, and scope revisions into internal-range work now re-prompt `Boundaries` instead of inheriting a stale generic answer, but broader Phase 1 human review on clarity/calmness and day-to-day trust is still open
- internal lateral-boundary scope-revision truth is now stronger because a LAN mission carrying `Report Adjacent Ranges Only` no longer drags that internal-only copy onto a revised web mission; the revised dossier now falls back to a compatible web boundary default, but broader Phase 1 human review on clarity/calmness and day-to-day trust is still open
- API scope-revision truth is now stronger because revising an API mission onto a plain website URL no longer keeps the mission type stuck on `API` or stores the revised target under `api_base_urls`; the revised dossier now reclassifies to `Website / Web App` and re-asks `Boundaries` before confirmation, but broader Phase 1 human review on clarity/calmness and day-to-day trust is still open
- non-internal boundary-copy truth is now stronger because once a revised route is no longer internal, the conversational `Boundaries` card and the Advanced Configuration boundary-notes placeholder no longer talk about `adjacent internal ranges` or expose the internal-only quick reply, but broader Phase 1 human review on clarity/calmness and day-to-day trust is still open
